## `ALB-5520-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ALB` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Off-Grid-PV-Inverter Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for RV Park & Campground Utility Hook-up
- **Intro:** Off-Grid-PV-Inverter Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for RV Park & Campground Utility Hook-up — Verify System Isolation and LOTO Status: Voltage reading must be 0 VDC and 0 VAC at all test points. Insulation resistance to ground must be ≥ 1 MΩ at 500 VDC.

- **Role:** Off-Grid-PV-Inverter Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for RV Park & Campground Utility Hook-up
